function ValverdsApp() {
  var _useState = useState('site'),
    _useState2 = _slicedToArray(_useState, 2),

    setCurrentView = _useState2[1]; // 'site' | 'reception' | 'client' | 'login'
  var _useState3 = useState('home'),
    _useState4 = _slicedToArray(_useState3, 2),

  // PERFIL ATIVO (RBAC HIER├üRQUICO)

    setCurrentUser = _useState6[1];

  // ABAS INTERNAS GERENCIAIS
  var _useState7 = useState('agenda'),
    _useState8 = _slicedToArray(_useState7, 2),

  var _useState9 = useState('minha_agenda'),

  var _useState13 = useState('Seg'),

  var scrollToSection = function scrollToSection(id) {

  // TROCA R├üPIDA DE PERFIL (RBAC EXECUTIVO)
  var switchRole = function switchRole(roleKey) {
    if (roleKey === 'dono') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Luis Valverde',
        email: 'luis@valverds.com',
        role: 'dono',
        barberKey: 'luis',
        title: 'Propriet├írio & Mestre Barbeiro',
        avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
      });
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setReceptionTab('agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'barbeiro') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Yago Barber',
        email: 'yago@valverds.com',
        role: 'barbeiro',
        barberKey: 'yago',
        title: 'Especialista em Fade & Estilo',
        avatar: 'yago_barber.png'
      });
      var yagoSched = barberSchedules.find(function (b) {
        return b.id === 'yago';
      }) || INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES[1];
      setBarberEditStartTime(yagoSched.startTime);
      setBarberEditEndTime(yagoSched.endTime);
      setBarberEditLunchBreak(yagoSched.lunchBreak);
      setBarberEditActiveDays(yagoSched.activeDays);
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setBarberTab('minha_agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'recepcao') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Recep├º├úo Valverd\'s',
        email: 'recepcao@valverds.com',
        role: 'recepcao',
        barberKey: 'recepcao',
        title: 'Gerente de Recep├º├úo & Caixa',
        avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
      });
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setReceptionTab('agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'cliente') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: clientProfile.name,
        email: clientProfile.email,
        role: 'cliente',
        barberKey: 'cliente',
        title: clientProfile.tier,
        avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
      });
      setCurrentView('client');
      setClientTab('agendamentos');
    }
  };
  var handleLoginSubmit = function handleLoginSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var email = loginEmail.toLowerCase();
    if (email.includes('luis')) switchRole('dono');else if (email.includes('yago')) switchRole('barbeiro');else if (email.includes('recepcao') || email.includes('admin')) switchRole('recepcao');else switchRole('cliente');
  };

  // M├ëTRICAS DO DIA
  var todaysAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return appointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined);
    });
  }, [appointments]);
  var completedToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'concluido';
  });
  var inChairToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'em_atendimento';
  });
  var waitingToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'aguardando';
  });
  var scheduledToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'agendado';
  });
  var revenueServices = completedToday.reduce(function (acc, a) {
    return acc + (a.total || a.price);
  }, 0);
  var revenueBalcao = counterSales.reduce(function (acc, s) {
    return acc + s.total;
  }, 0);
  var totalRevenueToday = revenueServices + revenueBalcao;

  var currentBarberName = currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? currentUser.name : currentUser.role === 'dono' ? 'Luis Valverde' : '';

      return a.barber === currentBarberName;

  // FUN├ç├òES DE A├ç├âO GERENCIAL
  var updateStock = function updateStock(id, delta) {
    setProducts(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (item) {
        return item.id === id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, item), {}, {
          stock: Math.max(0, item.stock + delta)
        }) : item;
      });
    });
  };
  var changeAppointmentStatus = function changeAppointmentStatus(id, newStatus) {
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, a), {}, {
          status: newStatus
        }) : a;
      });
    });
  };
  var deleteAppointment = function deleteAppointment(id) {
    if (confirm('Deseja realmente cancelar e remover este agendamento?')) {
      setAppointments(function (prev) {
        return prev.filter(function (a) {
          return a.id !== id;
        });
      });
    }
  };
  var openEditAppointment = function openEditAppointment(appt) {
    setEditingAppt(_objectSpread({}, appt));
    setIsEditApptModalOpen(true);
  };
  var handleSaveEditedAppt = function handleSaveEditedAppt(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === editingAppt.id ? editingAppt : a;
      });
    });
    setIsEditApptModalOpen(false);
  };
  var handleCreateBooking = function handleCreateBooking(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!bookingForm.client || !bookingForm.phone) {
      alert('Por favor preencha nome e WhatsApp.');
      return;
    }
    var srv = services.find(function (s) {
      return s.id === bookingForm.serviceId;
    }) || services[0];
    var brb = barbers.find(function (b) {
      return b.barberKey === bookingForm.barberKey;
    }) || barbers[0];
    var newAppt = {
      id: Date.now(),
      weekId: currentWeek.id,
      day: bookingForm.day,
      date: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? '08/09' : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? '09/09' : '10/09',
      dayNum: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? 8 : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? 9 : 10,
      client: bookingForm.client,
      phone: bookingForm.phone,
      service: srv.name,
      barber: brb.name,
      time: bookingForm.time,
      status: 'agendado',
      price: srv.price,
      items: [{
        name: srv.name,
        price: srv.price
      }],
      total: srv.price,
      paymentMethod: '',
      notes: bookingForm.notes || 'Agendado pelo painel.'
    };
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return [newAppt].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev));
    });
    setIsBookingModalOpen(false);
    setBookingForm({
      client: '',
      phone: '',
      serviceId: 's1',
      barberKey: 'luis',
      day: 'Seg',
      time: '14:00',
      notes: ''
    });
  };
  var openCheckout = function openCheckout(appt) {
    setCheckoutAppt(_objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, appt), {}, {
      items: _toConsumableArray(appt.items)
    }));
    setIsCheckoutModalOpen(true);
  };
  var addItemToCheckout = function addItemToCheckout(product) {
    if (!checkoutAppt || !product) return;
    if (product.price > 0 && product.stock <= 0) {
      alert('Item sem estoque dispon├¡vel!');
      return;
    }
    updateStock(product.id, -1);
    setCheckoutAppt(function (prev) {
      return _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, prev), {}, {
        items: [].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev.items), [{
          name: product.name,
          price: product.price
        }]),
        total: prev.total + product.price
      });
    });
  };
  var handleConfirmCheckout = function handleConfirmCheckout() {
    if (!checkoutAppt) return;
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === checkoutAppt.id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, a), {}, {
          status: 'concluido',
          items: checkoutAppt.items,
          total: checkoutAppt.total,
          paymentMethod: selectedPayment
        }) : a;
      });
    });
    setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false);
    setCheckoutAppt(null);
  };
  var handleQuickSale = function handleQuickSale(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!quickSaleProduct) return;
    var total = quickSaleProduct.price * quickSaleQty;
    updateStock(quickSaleProduct.id, -quickSaleQty);
    setCounterSales(function (prev) {
      return [{
        id: Date.now(),
        item: quickSaleProduct.name,
        category: quickSaleProduct.category,
        qty: quickSaleQty,
        total: total,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', {
          hour: '2-digit',
          minute: '2-digit'
        }),
        paymentMethod: quickSalePayment
      }].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev));
    });
    setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false);
  };
  var handleAddNewProduct = function handleAddNewProduct(e) {

    var newP = {

      category: newProductForm.category,
      price: parseFloat(newProductForm.price),
      stock: parseInt(newProductForm.stock),

      type: newProductForm.type
    };

      return [].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev), [newP]);
    });
    setIsNewProductModalOpen(false);
    setNewProductForm({
      name: '',
      category: 'Bar',
      price: 15,
      stock: 24,
      minStock: 8,
      type: 'Cerveja'
    });
  };
  var handleSaveBarberSchedule = function handleSaveBarberSchedule(e) {

      });

    setScheduleSavedToast(true);
    setTimeout(function () {
      return setScheduleSavedToast(false);
    }, 4000);
  };
  var toggleBarberDay = function toggleBarberDay(day) {
    if (barberEditActiveDays.includes(day)) {
      setBarberEditActiveDays(barberEditActiveDays.filter(function (d) {
        return d !== day;
      }));
    } else {
      setBarberEditActiveDays([].concat(_toConsumableArray(barberEditActiveDays), [day]));
    }
  };

  }, "Produto / Bebida:"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("select", {
    value: quickSaleProduct ? quickSaleProduct.id : '',
    onChange: function onChange(e) {
      return setQuickSaleProduct(products.find(function (p) {
        return p.id === e.target.value;
      }));
    },
    className: "w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"

  }))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

    type: "number",

    onChange: function onChange(e) {
      return setQuickSaleQty(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1);
    },
    className: "w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {
    className: "block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1"
  }, "Pagamento:"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("select", {
    value: quickSalePayment,
    onChange: function onChange(e) {
      return setQuickSalePayment(e.target.value);
    },
    className: "w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("option", {
    value: "PIX"
  }, "PIX"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("option", {

  }, "DINHEIRO")))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {

  }, "Total da Venda:"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("strong", {

  }, "R$ ", ((quickSaleProduct ? quickSaleProduct.price : 0) * quickSaleQty).toFixed(2).replace('.', ','))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "pt-2 flex gap-3"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    type: "button",

      return setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false);
    },
    className: "flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
  }, "Cancelar"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    type: "submit",

  }, "Finalizar Venda"))))), isNewProductModalOpen && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {

  }, "ESTOQUE & INVENT\xC1RIO"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("h3", {

  }, "Cadastrar Novo Produto")), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {

  }, "\u2715")), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("form", {

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {

  }, "Nome do Item:"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {

        name: 'Yago Barber',
        role: 'Especialista em Fade & Estilo',
        chair: 'Cadeira 02 (Fade & Style)',
        avatar: 'yago_barber.png',
        workDaysDesc: 'Ter├ºa a S├íbado (Folga Segunda)',
        activeDays: ['Ter', 'Qua', 'Qui', 'Sex', 'S├íb'],
        startTime: '10:00',
        endTime: '19:00',
        lunchBreak: '13:00 ├ás 14:00',
        slotDuration: '45 min',
        status: 'Dispon├¡vel Hoje',
        autoAccept: true
      }
    ];

    const TIME_SLOTS_COMPARE = [
      '09:00', '09:45', '10:30', '11:15', '12:00', '12:45', '13:30', '14:15', '15:00', '15:45', '16:30', '17:15', '18:00', '18:45'
    ];

    // =========================================================================
    // COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL: VALVERD'S BARBERSHOP APP
    // =========================================================================
    function ValverdsApp() {
      const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('site'); // 'site' | 'reception' | 'client' | 'login'
      const [activeNav, setActiveNav] = useState('home');

      // PERFIL ATIVO (RBAC HIER├üRQUICO)
      const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState({
        name: 'Luis Valverde',
        email: 'luis@valverds.com',
        role: 'dono', // 'dono' | 'barbeiro' | 'recepcao' | 'cliente'
        barberKey: 'luis',
        title: 'Propriet├írio & Mestre Barbeiro',
        avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
      });

      // ABAS INTERNAS GERENCIAIS
      const [receptionTab, setReceptionTab] = useState('agenda');
      const [barberTab, setBarberTab] = useState('minha_agenda');
      const [barberCalendarMode, setBarberCalendarMode] = useState('semanal');
      const [selectedWeekIndex, setSelectedWeekIndex] = useState(1);
      const [selectedWeekDay, setSelectedWeekDay] = useState('Seg');
      const [selectedMonthDay, setSelectedMonthDay] = useState(8);

      // ABAS CLIENTE
      const [clientTab, setClientTab] = useState('agendamentos');
      const [clientCalendarView, setClientCalendarView] = useState('semana');

      // DADOS VIVOS
      const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState(INITIAL_APPOINTMENTS);
      const [products, setProducts] = useState(INITIAL_PRODUCTS);
      const [services, setServices] = useState(INITIAL_SERVICES);
      const [barbers, setBarbers] = useState(INITIAL_BARBERS);
      const [shopHours, setShopHours] = useState(INITIAL_SHOP_HOURS);
      const [barberSchedules, setBarberSchedules] = useState(INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES);
      const [clientProfile, setClientProfile] = useState(INITIAL_CLIENT_PROFILE);

      // EDITOR DE ESCALA PELO BARBEIRO
      const [barberEditStartTime, setBarberEditStartTime] = useState('10:00');
      const [barberEditEndTime, setBarberEditEndTime] = useState('19:00');
      const [barberEditLunchBreak, setBarberEditLunchBreak] = useState('13:00 ├ás 14:00');
      const [barberEditActiveDays, setBarberEditActiveDays] = useState(['Ter', 'Qua', 'Qui', 'Sex', 'S├íb']);
      const [scheduleSavedToast, setScheduleSavedToast] = useState(false);

      // MODAIS DE A├ç├âO
      const [isBookingModalOpen, setIsBookingModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [isEditApptModalOpen, setIsEditApptModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [editingAppt, setEditingAppt] = useState(null);

      const [isCheckoutModalOpen, setIsCheckoutModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [checkoutAppt, setCheckoutAppt] = useState(null);
      const [selectedPayment, setSelectedPayment] = useState('PIX');
      const [additionalItemId, setAdditionalItemId] = useState('');

      const [isQuickSaleModalOpen, setIsQuickSaleModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [quickSaleProduct, setQuickSaleProduct] = useState(INITIAL_PRODUCTS[0]);
      const [quickSaleQty, setQuickSaleQty] = useState(1);
      const [quickSalePayment, setQuickSalePayment] = useState('PIX');

      const [isNewProductModalOpen, setIsNewProductModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [newProductForm, setNewProductForm] = useState({ name: '', category: 'Bar', price: 15, stock: 24, minStock: 8, type: 'Cerveja' });

      // FORMUL├üRIO DE NOVO AGENDAMENTO
      const [bookingForm, setBookingForm] = useState({
        client: '',
        phone: '',
        serviceId: 's1',
        barberKey: 'luis',
        day: 'Seg',
        time: '14:00',
        notes: ''
      });

      // VENDAS AVULSAS REGISTRADAS NO BALCAO
      const [counterSales, setCounterSales] = useState([
        { id: 901, item: 'Pomada Modeladora Matte Valverd\'s (150g)', category: 'Produtos', qty: 1, total: 45, time: '10:15', paymentMethod: 'PIX' },
        { id: 902, item: 'Cerveja IPA Artesanal Valverd\'s (500ml)', category: 'Bar', qty: 2, total: 40, time: '11:20', paymentMethod: 'CART├âO' }
      ]);

      const [loginEmail, setLoginEmail] = useState('');
      const [loginPassword, setLoginPassword] = useState('');
      const [currentSlide, setCurrentSlide] = useState(0);

      const currentWeek = WEEKS_DATA[selectedWeekIndex] || WEEKS_DATA[1];
      const nextSlide = () => setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev + 1) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length);
      const prevSlide = () => setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev - 1 + GALLERY_SLIDES.length) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length);

      const scrollToSection = (id) => {
        setActiveNav(id);
        if (currentView !== 'site') setCurrentView('site');
        const element = document.getElementById(id);
        if (element) element.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      };

      // TROCA R├üPIDA DE PERFIL (RBAC EXECUTIVO)
      const switchRole = (roleKey) => {
        if (roleKey === 'dono') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Luis Valverde',
            email: 'luis@valverds.com',
            role: 'dono',
            barberKey: 'luis',
            title: 'Propriet├írio & Mestre Barbeiro',
            avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
          });
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setReceptionTab('agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'barbeiro') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Yago Barber',
            email: 'yago@valverds.com',
            role: 'barbeiro',
            barberKey: 'yago',
            title: 'Especialista em Fade & Estilo',
            avatar: 'yago_barber.png'
          });
          const yagoSched = barberSchedules.find(b => b.id === 'yago') || INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES[1];
          setBarberEditStartTime(yagoSched.startTime);
          setBarberEditEndTime(yagoSched.endTime);
          setBarberEditLunchBreak(yagoSched.lunchBreak);
          setBarberEditActiveDays(yagoSched.activeDays);
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setBarberTab('minha_agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'recepcao') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Recep├º├úo Valverd\'s',
            email: 'recepcao@valverds.com',
            role: 'recepcao',
            barberKey: 'recepcao',
            title: 'Gerente de Recep├º├úo & Caixa',
            avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
          });
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setReceptionTab('agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'cliente') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: clientProfile.name,
            email: clientProfile.email,
            role: 'cliente',
            barberKey: 'cliente',
            title: clientProfile.tier,
            avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
          });
          setCurrentView('client');
          setClientTab('agendamentos');
        }
      };

      const handleLoginSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const email = loginEmail.toLowerCase();
        if (email.includes('luis')) switchRole('dono');
        else if (email.includes('yago')) switchRole('barbeiro');
        else if (email.includes('recepcao') || email.includes('admin')) switchRole('recepcao');
        else switchRole('cliente');
      };

      // M├ëTRICAS DO DIA
      const todaysAppointments = useMemo(() => {
        return appointments.filter(a => a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined));
      }, [appointments]);

      const completedToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'concluido');
      const inChairToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'em_atendimento');
      const waitingToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'aguardando');
      const scheduledToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'agendado');

      const revenueServices = completedToday.reduce((acc, a) => acc + (a.total || a.price), 0);
      const revenueBalcao = counterSales.reduce((acc, s) => acc + s.total, 0);
      const totalRevenueToday = revenueServices + revenueBalcao;

      // M├ëTRICAS DO BARBEIRO
      const currentBarberName = currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? currentUser.name : (currentUser.role === 'dono' ? 'Luis Valverde' : '');
      const myAllAppointments = useMemo(() => appointments.filter(a => a.barber === currentBarberName), [appointments, currentBarberName]);
      const myWeekAppointments = useMemo(() => myAllAppointments.filter(a => a.weekId === currentWeek.id || (currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined)), [myAllAppointments, currentWeek]);
      const myTodaysAppointments = useMemo(() => myAllAppointments.filter(a => a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined)), [myAllAppointments]);
      const myCompletedToday = myTodaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'concluido');
      const myRevenueToday = myCompletedToday.reduce((acc, a) => acc + a.price, 0);
      const myCommissionRate = currentUser.name.includes('Luis') ? 0.60 : 0.50;
      const myCommissionToday = myRevenueToday * myCommissionRate;

      // FUN├ç├òES DE A├ç├âO GERENCIAL
      const updateStock = (id, delta) => {
        setProducts(prev => prev.map(item => item.id === id ? { ...item, stock: Math.max(0, item.stock + delta) } : item));
      };

      const changeAppointmentStatus = (id, newStatus) => {
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === id ? { ...a, status: newStatus } : a));
      };

      const deleteAppointment = (id) => {
        if (confirm('Deseja realmente cancelar e remover este agendamento?')) {
          setAppointments(prev => prev.filter(a => a.id !== id));
        }
      };

      const openEditAppointment = (appt) => {
        setEditingAppt({ ...appt });
        setIsEditApptModalOpen(true);
      };

      const handleSaveEditedAppt = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === editingAppt.id ? editingAppt : a));
        setIsEditApptModalOpen(false);
      };

      const handleCreateBooking = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!bookingForm.client || !bookingForm.phone) {
          alert('Por favor preencha nome e WhatsApp.');
          return;
        }

        const srv = services.find(s => s.id === bookingForm.serviceId) || services[0];
        const brb = barbers.find(b => b.barberKey === bookingForm.barberKey) || barbers[0];

        const newAppt = {
          id: Date.now(),
          weekId: currentWeek.id,
          day: bookingForm.day,
          date: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? '08/09' : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? '09/09' : '10/09',
          dayNum: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? 8 : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? 9 : 10,
          client: bookingForm.client,
          phone: bookingForm.phone,
          service: srv.name,
          barber: brb.name,
          time: bookingForm.time,
          status: 'agendado',
          price: srv.price,
          items: [{ name: srv.name, price: srv.price }],
          total: srv.price,
          paymentMethod: '',
          notes: bookingForm.notes || 'Agendado pelo painel.'
        };

        setAppointments(prev => [newAppt, ...prev]);
        setIsBookingModalOpen(false);
        setBookingForm({ client: '', phone: '', serviceId: 's1', barberKey: 'luis', day: 'Seg', time: '14:00', notes: '' });
      };

      const openCheckout = (appt) => {
        setCheckoutAppt({ ...appt, items: [...appt.items] });
        setIsCheckoutModalOpen(true);
      };

      const addItemToCheckout = (product) => {
        if (!checkoutAppt || !product) return;
        if (product.price > 0 && product.stock <= 0) {
          alert('Item sem estoque dispon├¡vel!');
          return;
        }
        updateStock(product.id, -1);
        setCheckoutAppt(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          items: [...prev.items, { name: product.name, price: product.price }],
          total: prev.total + product.price
        }));
      };

      const handleConfirmCheckout = () => {
        if (!checkoutAppt) return;
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === checkoutAppt.id ? {
          ...a,
          status: 'concluido',
          items: checkoutAppt.items,
          total: checkoutAppt.total,
          paymentMethod: selectedPayment
        } : a));
        setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false);
        setCheckoutAppt(null);
      };

      const handleQuickSale = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!quickSaleProduct) return;
        const total = quickSaleProduct.price * quickSaleQty;
        updateStock(quickSaleProduct.id, -quickSaleQty);
        setCounterSales(prev => [
          {
            id: Date.now(),
            item: quickSaleProduct.name,
            category: quickSaleProduct.category,
            qty: quickSaleQty,
            total,
            time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
            paymentMethod: quickSalePayment
          },
          ...prev
        ]);
        setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false);
      };

      const handleAddNewProduct = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const newP = {
          id: 'p' + Date.now(),
          name: newProductForm.name,
          category: newProductForm.category,
          price: parseFloat(newProductForm.price),
          stock: parseInt(newProductForm.stock),
          minStock: parseInt(newProductForm.minStock),
          type: newProductForm.type
        };
        setProducts(prev => [...prev, newP]);
        setIsNewProductModalOpen(false);
        setNewProductForm({ name: '', category: 'Bar', price: 15, stock: 24, minStock: 8, type: 'Cerveja' });
      };

      const handleSaveBarberSchedule = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const currentBarberId = currentUser.barberKey || 'yago';
        setBarberSchedules(prev => prev.map(b => b.id === currentBarberId ? {
          ...b,
          startTime: barberEditStartTime,
          endTime: barberEditEndTime,
          lunchBreak: barberEditLunchBreak,
          activeDays: barberEditActiveDays,
          workDaysDesc: `${barberEditActiveDays.join(', ')} (${barberEditStartTime} ├ás ${barberEditEndTime})`
        } : b));
        setScheduleSavedToast(true);
        setTimeout(() => setScheduleSavedToast(false), 4000);
      };

      const toggleBarberDay = (day) => {
        if (barberEditActiveDays.includes(day)) {
          setBarberEditActiveDays(barberEditActiveDays.filter(d => d !== day));
        } else {
          setBarberEditActiveDays([...barberEditActiveDays, day]);
        }
      };

      // STATUS BADGE DARK LUXURY
      const StatusBadge = ({ status }) => {
        const styles = {
          concluido: 'bg-emerald-950/70 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-800/60',
          em_atendimento: 'bg-blue-950/70 text-blue-300 border-blue-800/60',
          aguardando: 'bg-amber-950/70 text-amber-300 border-amber-800/60',
          agendado: 'bg-[#1E1D19] text-[#A8A397] border-[#2A261F]'
        };
        const labels = {
          concluido: 'Conclu├¡do',
          em_atendimento: 'Na Cadeira',
          aguardando: 'No Lounge',
          agendado: 'Agendado'
        };
        return (
          <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-serif font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border ${styles[status] || styles.agendado}`}>
            <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full mr-1.5 ${status === 'concluido' ? 'bg-emerald-400' : status === 'em_atendimento' ? 'bg-blue-400' : status === 'aguardando' ? 'bg-amber-400' : 'bg-[#C9A356]'}`}></span>
            {labels[status] || status}
          </span>
        );
      };

      // SELETOR DE PERFIL DEMO NO TOPO (P├ìLULA VINTAGE GOLD)
      const DemoRoleSwitcher = () => (
        <div className="bg-[#191713] border border-[#3A3326] rounded-full p-1 flex items-center gap-1 shadow-2xl">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold px-2.5 hidden sm:inline">
            ÔÜí PERFIL DEMO:
          </span>
          <button
            onClick={() => switchRole('dono')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1 ${currentUser.role === 'dono' && currentView === 'reception' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#25221B]'}`}
            title="Dono: Acesso irrestrito a DRE, todas as cadeiras e equipe"
          >
            <span>­ƒææ</span><span>Dono</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => switchRole('barbeiro')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1 ${currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#25221B]'}`}
            title="Barbeiro (Yago): Minha Agenda, Meus Ganhos, Minha Escala"
          >
            <span>Ô£é´©Å</span><span>Barbeiro</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => switchRole('recepcao')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1 ${currentUser.role === 'recepcao' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#25221B]'}`}
            title="Recep├º├úo: Fila do Sof├í e Encaixes"
          >
            <span>­ƒôï</span><span>Recep├º├úo</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => switchRole('cliente')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1 ${currentView === 'client' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#25221B]'}`}
            title="Cliente VIP: Cart├úo Fidelidade e Agendamento"
          >
            <span>­ƒæñ</span><span>Cliente</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      );

      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#0B0B0B] text-[#E0DDD5] font-sans antialiased flex flex-col selection:bg-[#C9A356] selection:text-black">
          
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODO 1: SITE P├ÜBLICO DE APRESENTA├ç├âO (ESTILO DARK LUXURY CL├üSSICO)         */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {currentView === 'site' && (
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row min-h-screen">
              
              {/* SIDEBAR VERTICAL ESQUERDA FIXA */}
              <aside className="w-full md:w-[145px] md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:left-0 bg-[#0B0B0B] border-r border-[#211F1C] z-40 flex md:flex-col items-center justify-between p-3 md:py-6 text-white shadow-2xl">
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center cursor-pointer mb-2" onClick={() => scrollToSection('home')}>
                  <OfficialLogo className="w-20 h-20" />
                  <span className="text-[9px] font-serif tracking-[0.25em] text-[#C9A356] mt-1 font-bold">EST. 2022</span>
                </div>

                <nav className="w-full flex md:flex-col items-center justify-around md:justify-center gap-1 md:gap-2.5 overflow-x-auto md:overflow-visible py-1">
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('home')} className={`flex flex-col items-center p-2 rounded w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'home' ? 'text-[#C9A356]' : 'text-[#E0DDD5] hover:text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                    <BowtieIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-semibold mt-1">HOME</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('sobre-nos')} className={`flex flex-col items-center p-2 rounded w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'sobre-nos' ? 'text-[#C9A356]' : 'text-[#E0DDD5] hover:text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                    <BarberBeardIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-semibold mt-1 text-center">SOBRE</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('espaco')} className={`flex flex-col items-center p-2 rounded w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'espaco' ? 'text-[#C9A356]' : 'text-[#E0DDD5] hover:text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                    <CrossedRazorsIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-semibold mt-1">ESPA├çO</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('servicos')} className={`flex flex-col items-center p-2 rounded w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'servicos' ? 'text-[#C9A356]' : 'text-[#E0DDD5] hover:text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                    <ClassicShearsIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-semibold mt-1">SERVI├çOS</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('bebidas')} className={`flex flex-col items-center p-2 rounded w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'bebidas' ? 'text-[#C9A356]' : 'text-[#E0DDD5] hover:text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                    <CombAndRazorIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-semibold mt-1">BAR</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('barbeiros')} className={`flex flex-col items-center p-2 rounded w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'barbeiros' ? 'text-[#C9A356]' : 'text-[#E0DDD5] hover:text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                    <CuttingScissorsIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-semibold mt-1">EQUIPE</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('localizacao')} className={`flex flex-col items-center p-2 rounded w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'localizacao' ? 'text-[#C9A356]' : 'text-[#E0DDD5] hover:text-[#C9A356]'}`}>
                    <MapPinIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-[#C9A356]" />
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-serif tracking-wider uppercase font-semibold mt-1 text-center">RESENDE</span>
                  </button>
                </nav>

                <div className="w-full pt-3 border-t border-[#211F1C] flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => switchRole('cliente')}
                    className="w-full py-2 px-1 rounded-lg bg-[#C9A356]/15 hover:bg-[#C9A356] border border-[#C9A356]/40 text-[#C9A356] hover:text-black transition-all flex flex-col items-center text-center shadow"
                  >
                    <span className="text-xs">­ƒæñ</span>
                    <span className="text-[8px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">├üREA CLIENTE</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => switchRole('dono')}
                    className="w-full py-2 px-1 rounded-lg bg-[#161512] hover:bg-[#201E19] border border-[#2A261F] text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-all flex flex-col items-center text-center"
                  >
                    <span className="text-xs">­ƒöÆ</span>
                    <span className="text-[8px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">EQUIPE / LOGIN</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </aside>

                                          ­ƒøï´©Å Chegou

                                        </button>

                                      {/* BOT├âO EDITAR */}

'bg-[#C9A356] text-black border-[#C9A356] shadow-xl scale-105 font-bold' : d.isOpen ? 'bg-[#1C1A14] text-gray-200 border-[#2C261D] hover:border-[#C9A356]/50' : 'bg-[#12110E] text-gray-600 border-transparent cursor-not-allowed'}`}

                                  {TIME_SLOTS_COMPARE.map((slot) => {

                                    const isLuisLunch = slot === '12:45' || slot === '13:30';
                                    const isYagoLunch = slot === '13:30' || slot === '14:15';

                                    return (

                      </div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between py-2 border-b border-[#282319]">
                        <span className="text-gray-400">Servi├ºo Mais Pedido:</span>
                        <strong className="text-white">{clientProfile.preferredService}</strong>
                      </div>
                      <div className="py-2 border-b border-[#282319]">
                        <span className="text-gray-400 block mb-1">Observa├º├Áes do seu Corte & Barba:</span>
                        <p className="text-white font-medium bg-[#12110E] p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#2A2419]">
                          "{clientProfile.styleNotes}"
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        setBookingForm(prev => ({ ...prev, barberKey: 'luis', serviceId: 's1' }));
                        setIsBookingModalOpen(true);
                      }}
                      className="w-full py-3.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider rounded-2xl transition-all shadow"
                    >
                      ÔÜí Repetir Meu ├Ültimo Corte com Luis Valverde
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}

              </main>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: NOVO AGENDAMENTO / ENCAIXE                                         */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isBookingModalOpen && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 sm:p-8 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-5">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-4 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">AGENDAMENTO & ENCAIXE</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white mt-1">Marcar Hor├írio</h3>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsBookingModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleCreateBooking} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Nome do Cliente:</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={bookingForm.client}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, client: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="Ex: Carlos Eduardo"
                        className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C9A356]"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">WhatsApp:</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={bookingForm.phone}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, phone: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="(21) 98888-7777"
                        className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C9A356]"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Servi├ºo:</label>
                    <select
                      value={bookingForm.serviceId}
                      onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, serviceId: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-[#C9A356]"
                    >
                      {services.map(s => (
                        <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name} ÔÇö R$ {s.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Barbeiro:</label>
                      <select
                        value={bookingForm.barberKey}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, barberKey: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        {barbers.map(b => (
                          <option key={b.id} value={b.barberKey}>{b.name.split(' ')[0]}</option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Dia:</label>
                      <select
                        value={bookingForm.day}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, day: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="Seg">Segunda (08/09)</option>
                        <option value="Ter">Ter├ºa (09/09)</option>
                        <option value="Qua">Quarta (10/09)</option>
                        <option value="Qui">Quinta (11/09)</option>
                        <option value="Sex">Sexta (12/09)</option>
                        <option value="S├íb">S├íbado (13/09)</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Hor├írio:</label>
                      <select
                        value={bookingForm.time}
                        onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, time: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        {TIME_SLOTS_COMPARE.map(t => (
                          <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Observa├º├Áes:</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={bookingForm.notes}
                      onChange={e => setBookingForm({ ...bookingForm, notes: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Ex: Degrad├¬ cl├íssico, toalha quente..."
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-white text-xs focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-3 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsBookingModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-3 bg-[#1F1C16] hover:bg-[#2A261F] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="flex-1 py-3 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider rounded-xl transition-all shadow-xl"
                    >
                      Confirmar Hor├írio
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: EDITAR AGENDAMENTO (EDI├ç├âO DIRETA)                                 */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isEditApptModalOpen && editingAppt && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-3 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">GERENCIAMENTO</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white mt-0.5">Editar Agendamento</h3>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsEditApptModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl font-bold">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleSaveEditedAppt} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Nome do Cliente:</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={editingAppt.client}
                      onChange={e => setEditingAppt({ ...editingAppt, client: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-white"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Hor├írio:</label>
                      <select
                        value={editingAppt.time}
                        onChange={e => setEditingAppt({ ...editingAppt, time: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                      >
                        {TIME_SLOTS_COMPARE.map(t => <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>)}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Status:</label>
                      <select
                        value={editingAppt.status}
                        onChange={e => setEditingAppt({ ...editingAppt, status: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-white"
                      >
                        <option value="agendado">Agendado</option>
                        <option value="aguardando">No Lounge</option>
                        <option value="em_atendimento">Na Cadeira</option>
                        <option value="concluido">Conclu├¡do</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Observa├º├Áes:</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={editingAppt.notes}
                      onChange={e => setEditingAppt({ ...editingAppt, notes: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsEditApptModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all shadow"
                    >
                      Salvar Altera├º├Áes
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: FECHAMENTO DE COMANDA / CHECKOUT                                   */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isCheckoutModalOpen && checkoutAppt && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 sm:p-8 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-5">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-4 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">FECHAMENTO DE COMANDA</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white mt-1">{checkoutAppt.client}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{checkoutAppt.service} com {checkoutAppt.barber}</p>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div className="bg-[#1C1A14] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#2D271E] space-y-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-[#C9A356] uppercase tracking-wider block text-[10px]">ITENS CONSUMIDOS:</span>
                    {checkoutAppt.items.map((item, idx) => (
                      <div key={idx} className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-[#262118] last:border-none">
                        <span className="text-gray-200">{item.name}</span>
                        <strong className="font-serif text-[#C9A356]">R$ {item.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}</strong>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1 font-medium">+ Adicionar Bebida ou Pomada:</label>
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <select
                        value={additionalItemId}
                        onChange={e => setAdditionalItemId(e.target.value)}
                        className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                      >
                        <option value="">Selecione um item do bar ou produto...</option>
                        {products.filter(p => p.stock > 0).map(p => (
                          <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name} ÔÇö R$ {p.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')} (Estoque: {p.stock})</option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          const prod = products.find(p => p.id === additionalItemId);
                          addItemToCheckout(prod);
                        }}
                        className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#25221B] hover:bg-[#C9A356] hover:text-black text-[#C9A356] font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all"
                      >
                        Adicionar
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-4 bg-[#1C1A14] rounded-2xl border border-green-500/30 flex justify-between items-center">
                    <span className="font-bold text-white text-sm uppercase">Total da Comanda:</span>
                    <strong className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-green-400">
                      R$ {checkoutAppt.total.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}
                    </strong>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1 font-medium">Forma de Pagamento:</label>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                      {['PIX', 'CART├âO', 'DINHEIRO'].map(m => (
                        <button
                          key={m}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setSelectedPayment(m)}
                          className={`py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs font-serif transition-all ${selectedPayment === m ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300'}`}
                        >
                          {m}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-3 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleConfirmCheckout}
                      className="flex-1 py-3 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider rounded-xl shadow-xl"
                    >
                      Confirmar Pagamento
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: VENDA R├üPIDA DE BALC├âO                                             */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isQuickSaleModalOpen && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-3 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">PDV BALC├âO</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white mt-1">Venda Avulsa de Produto</h3>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl font-bold">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleQuickSale} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Produto / Bebida:</label>
                    <select
                      value={quickSaleProduct ? quickSaleProduct.id : ''}
                      onChange={e => setQuickSaleProduct(products.find(p => p.id === e.target.value))}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                    >
                      {products.map(p => (
                        <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name} ÔÇö R$ {p.price.toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')} ({p.stock} un)</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Quantidade:</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        max={quickSaleProduct ? quickSaleProduct.stock : 10}
                        value={quickSaleQty}
                        onChange={e => setQuickSaleQty(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Pagamento:</label>
                      <select
                        value={quickSalePayment}
                        onChange={e => setQuickSalePayment(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                      >
                        <option value="PIX">PIX</option>
                        <option value="CART├âO">CART├âO</option>
                        <option value="DINHEIRO">DINHEIRO</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-4 bg-[#1C1A14] rounded-2xl border border-[#2A261F] flex justify-between items-center">
                    <span className="font-bold text-white uppercase">Total da Venda:</span>
                    <strong className="font-serif text-2xl text-green-400">
                      R$ {((quickSaleProduct ? quickSaleProduct.price : 0) * quickSaleQty).toFixed(2).replace('.', ',')}
                    </strong>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all shadow"
                    >
                      Finalizar Venda
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODAL: NOVO PRODUTO NO ESTOQUE                                            */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {isNewProductModalOpen && (
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
              <div className="bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start pb-3 border-b border-[#2A261F]">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold">ESTOQUE & INVENT├üRIO</span>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-bold text-white mt-1">Cadastrar Novo Produto</h3>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => setIsNewProductModalOpen(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xl font-bold">Ô£ò</button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleAddNewProduct} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Nome do Item:</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={newProductForm.name}
                      onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, name: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Ex: Cerveja Stella Artois (330ml)"
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Categoria:</label>
                      <select
                        value={newProductForm.category}
                        onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, category: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                      >
                        <option value="Bar">Bar</option>
                        <option value="Produtos">Produtos</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Pre├ºo de Venda (R$):</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        step="0.50"
                        required
                        value={newProductForm.price}
                        onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, price: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Estoque Inicial:</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        value={newProductForm.stock}
                        onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, stock: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-gray-400 font-serif uppercase mb-1">Estoque M├¡nimo:</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        value={newProductForm.minStock}
                        onChange={e => setNewProductForm({ ...newProductForm, minStock: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-[#0B0B0B] border border-[#2A261F] rounded-xl text-xs text-white font-bold"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setIsNewProductModalOpen(false)}
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#1F1C16] text-gray-300 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase rounded-xl transition-all shadow"
                    >
                      Salvar no Estoque
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>
      );
    }

    const root = ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root'));
    root.render(<ValverdsApp />);
  </script>

</body></html>
